<div class="col col12">
<h2>
<span class="inner-txt no_subheader">
<a href="https://www.neakriti.gr/category/ygeia/">
					Υγεία
				</a>

<div class="articleAbsCaption">

                    ΥΓΕΙΑ

            require('ads', function(){
			    googletag.display(divId);
            });
	});
	</script>
